# 1684 new apps — 2026-11-19

**Anomaly:** 1684 new apps is 205% of the median of the last 7 reports (820). Check for duplicates or a change in what Apple returns.

Window: apps first found between 2026-11-18 19:23 UTC (20:23 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-19 19:50 UTC (20:50 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-17 19:23 UTC (20:23 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span. Date-only release dates count by calendar day in America/Los_Angeles. Storefronts: us.
